## InputAdmission (E1)

`Stage2CandidateSetForStage3` admitted at the boundary (classified/bound, NOT persisted).
`derivedFromStage2CandidateID` = the admitted Stage-2 candidate id = ancestry. Read the SET, not its
cardinality: **498 is this run's count, not a rule**.

## The ordering decision (render-only, score-free structural) — identity_sequence_ordering (E4)

**RelationEdgeTable** manufactured over the admitted set, then split:

- **PromoteEdge** (EvidenceSupport AND DirectionClarity AND RoleCompatibility AND StageCompatibility):
  **863** edges of two kinds —
  - `AdjacentBackbone` (497): the corpus's own exposition
    order is the forward direction of the knowledge chain — each chapter's constructs presuppose the
    preceding chapter's (1장 정의층 -> 2장 철학·반응층 -> 3장 인재 역량·역할 -> 4장 봇 사회화·측정 ->
    5장 TRB 의미·측정 -> 6장 TRB의 AX 확장 -> 7장 컨텍스트·지식행동사슬 실행층 -> 8장 예측지능·ESG 확장).
  - `ParentEnablesFragment` (366): a Split parent
    directly enables each of its promoted fragments, so the parent is ordered immediately before its own
    children. This is the containment relation Stage 2 recorded as `fragmentedFrom`, read forward.
- **HardRejectEdge**: **0** — no admitted pair stands in a contradictory or stage-incompatible relation
  (the contradictory/ungrounded candidates were already removed at Stage 2 as Reject or held at ManualReview).
- **ForwardBackbone**: all 863 PromoteEdges (acyclic).
- **FeedbackLoopSet**: **0** back-references recorded and EXCLUDED from the topological order —
  candidates the corpus introduces in one chapter and completes in another (deferral / reuse). They are
  ordered once, at their defining position; the back-reference is not a second ordering.
- **TopologicalOrder** computed over the ForwardBackbone only.

## FeedbackLoopSet (recorded, not ordered twice)

**|FeedbackLoopSet| = 0.**

This is a genuine structural result, not an unexamined default. The admitted set was swept for the corpus's
own deferral/reuse language ("full treatment deferred to N장", "introduced in N장, defined here", "ch7
construct reused here") — which is what a knowledge-chain back-edge looks like in this corpus. **Every such
row had already been resolved upstream at Stage 1**, so none survives into C1:

| Stage-1 row | candidate | Stage1Status | resolution |
|---|---|---|---|
| S1C-062 | `TEAM_ROLE_BALANCE` (ch4, "full treatment deferred to 5장") | COLLAPSE | collapsed onto S1C-090, the 5장 defining occurrence |
| S1C-087 | `AUGMENTED_ROLE_BALANCE_INDEX` (ch4, "full treatment deferred to 6장") | COLLAPSE | collapsed onto S1C-112 `ARBI`, the 6장 defining occurrence |
| S1C-113 | `AHCI` (ch6, "introduced in 4장, defined here") | COLLAPSE | collapsed onto S1C-086, the 4장 defining occurrence |
| S1C-184 | `KNOWLEDGE_ACTION_CHAIN` (ch8, "ch7 construct reused here") | COLLAPSE | collapsed onto S1C-133, the 7장 defining occurrence |
| S1C-089 | `COMMON_AND_GOVERNANCE_CONTEXT` (ch4 pairing) | MANUAL | Stage-2 Reject (ParentDuplicate) |

Because candidate_adjudication merged each deferral pair onto its single defining occurrence, no ADMITTED
candidate is introduced in one chapter and completed in another. The ForwardBackbone is therefore acyclic
with no back-edge to withhold, and the TopologicalOrder covers the whole admitted set.

**resolvable links**; NO bare name is handed to Stage 4. `precedes`/`follows` are the forward/backward
directional RELATION derived from the ForwardBackbone edges (parent->fragment containment included), and are
distinct from the `sequencePrevious`/`sequenceNext` neighbour pointers.

| [S3S-0001 AX (AI Transformation)](#s3s-0001) | 1장 — AX 패러다임과 조직 OS의 정의층. 이후 모든 후보가 전제하는 최상위 맥락을 먼저 확정한다. 이 후보는 해당 층의 독립 노드로, 선행 층의 구성물을 입력으로 받아 후속 층에 전달한다. |
